No prior experience is needed an  <https://london.sciencegallery.com/blog/creativity-and-wellbeing-week-2026-celebrating-creative-health>

- Born from the Heart, Photos from the Soul (until 20 May) shares photographs and words created by mothers using Photovoice, a participatory approach combining photography and storytelling, to look beyond clinical encounters and consider the wider experiences shaping maternity journeys.   <https://london.sciencegallery.com/blog/creativity-and-wellbeing-week-2026-celebrating-creative-health>
- A Thread That Binds Us (until 31 July) presents two new tapestry-based works by Amber Roper, commissioned by Bethlem Gallery, created in response to research workshops exploring intergenerational mental health and parents' beliefs and concerns about mental wellbeing in families. https://www.english-heritage.org.uk/visit/places/the-cenotaph/
- The Cenotaph – or empty tomb' in Greek – has been the focus of our nation's remembrance for a century. Initially a temporary monument designed by Edwin Lutyens in 1919, the Cenotaph in Whitehall was replaced with the permanent Portland stone memorial and unveiled on Armistice Day, 11 November 1920. The memorial became a central point for all those whose family and friends had died during the First  <https://www.english-heritage.org.uk/visit/places/the-cenotaph/>

## Barnet Market (market)
https://barnet-market.co.uk/
- Chipping Barnet has had a market as part of the town since the 1100 s and possibly even before that. The word Chipping means market town and on 23 August 1199 King John issued a Charter for a Market at Barnet to the Lord of the Manor, the Abbot of St. Albans, John de Cella. This charter is one of the oldest recorded for a market.   <https://barnet-market.co.uk/>
- On 6 February 1588 a new charter for Barnet Market was issued by Queen Elizabeth I to the then Lord of the Manor, Charles Butler, which also allowed the holding of the very popular Barnet Horse Fair . The market has had several locations in and around High Barnet, including a market once being established around the St John the Baptist church which itself sits on the junction of Wood street and Ba  <https://barnet-market.co.uk/>

## Thames Barrier Park (park)
https://www.london.gov.uk/programmes-strategies/environment-and-climate-change/parks-green-spaces-and-biodiversity/parks-and-green-spaces/thames-barrier-park
- London s New Year s Eve 2026   <https://www.london.gov.uk/programmes-strategies/environment-and-climate-change/parks-green-spaces-and-biodiversity/parks-and-green-spaces/thames-barrier-park>
- St Patrick’s Festival 2027   <https://www.london.gov.uk/programmes-strategies/environment-and-climate-change/parks-green-spaces-and-biodiversity/parks-and-green-spaces/thames-barrier-park>
- The HSBC Championships 2026 returns to the Historic Queen's Club in West Kensington, London, running from 6-21 June 2026 for two weeks of world-class grass-court tennis. One of the sport's most iconic pre-Wimbledon tournaments, the event features both ATP 500 men's competition and WTA 500 women's competition, attracting top international talent and British Favourites alike.   <https://www.london.gov.uk/programmes-strategies/sport/major-sports-events-london>
- The England and Wales Cricket Board is hosting the ICC Women's T20 World Cup in June 2026 with the final being held in London on 5th July at the Lords Cricket Ground. The tournament will have twelve (12) competing nations, with 33 matches played across six to eight Host Cities.   <https://www.london.gov.uk/programmes-strategies/sport/major-sports-events-london>
- London will once again host three regular-season games - the only city in the world to welcome multiple fixtures in 2026. The Jacksonville Jaguars will return for two games, split between their long-standing home at Wembley stadium and second fixture at Tottenham Hotspur Stadium. The Washington Commanders will host the third London game, also at Tottenham, marking another key moment in the NFL's d  <https://www.london.gov.uk/programmes-strategies/sport/major-sports-events-london>
- Tour de France Femmes avec Zwift 2027   <https://www.london.gov.uk/programmes-strategies/sport/major-sports-events-london>
- London is hosting the final stage of the Grand Depart of the Tour De France Femmes avec Zwift in summer 2027   <https://www.london.gov.uk/programmes-strategies/sport/major-sports-events-london>
- In summer 2027, London will host the Men’s and Women’s EuroHockey Championships. The preliminary rounds and quarterfinals will be held at the Lee Valley Hockey and Tennis Centre and the semi-finals and finals’ at The Stoop. This tournament will debut an all-knockout format featuring 12 competing teams.   <https://www.london.gov.uk/programmes-strategies/sport/major-sports-events-london>
- Come and celebrate London’s St Patrick’s Day Festival on Sunday 14 March 2027 in Trafalgar Square.   <https://www.london.gov.uk/events>
- BMW Classics, the London Symphony Orchestra’s free outdoor summer concert in Trafalgar Square, will next take place on Saturday 13 June 2026 – conducted by LSO Chief Conductor Sir Antonio Pappano.   <https://www.london.gov.uk/events/bmw-classics-2026>
- In 2026 and 2027, the LSO launches an ambitious multi-platform celebration of Holst’s The Planets, with BMW Classics serving as the project’s largest live-performance moment. Audiences in Trafalgar Square and online will get to hear five movements of the astrological-inspired suite — Mars, Mercury, Jupiter, Venus and Uranus.   <https://www.london.gov.uk/events/bmw-classics-2026>

Timed talks 12, 1.30 2.30 3.30 dancing fun creative crafts Oh! artists’ studios are open too! Friends of Valentines Mansion (@fovm.bsky.social) 2026-03-19T08:14:50.717Z   <https://valentines.org.uk/>

- Whilst the resident artists have a theme for Summer, the Winter Exhibition ( 21 Nov 26 to 11 Jan 27) gives them the opportunity to create a piece of their own choosing. This curated collection included paintings, ceramics, sculptures, photography and textiles – and, although a ‘freestyle’ show, each of the new works has a story to tell.   <https://valentines.org.uk/events/exhibitions-in-the-gallery/>
- The 2026 Summer Exhibition ( 19 Jul to 24 Aug) was on the theme of Metamorphosis: Life cycles, decay, transformation and change in nature . A curated collection of paintings, sketches, jewellery, sculptures, photographs and basketry. The pieces created specifically for this show by the resident artists and each represented a very different interpretation of the subject matter.   <https://valentines.org.uk/events/exhibitions-in-the-gallery/>
- From 24 May to 6 July. Ordinary Days an exhibition of work by a group of extremely talented young artists, aged 8 to 18. All members of a local club, ARTYGalina. Back by popular demand. Nurtured by their very gifted teacher and local resident, Galina Rafilailova, the group produced an exceptional collection of mixed media paintings. The youngsters explore a variety of disciplines including pencil   <https://valentines.org.uk/events/exhibitions-in-the-gallery/>
- 25 Jan to 1 Mar 2026. An exhibition showing works by Stanislaw Brunstein , born in Warsaw in 1914, died in Ilford in 1994.   <https://valentines.org.uk/events/exhibitions-in-the-gallery/>

## The Valley (stadium)
https://www.cafc.co.uk/
- Be part of the second half of Charlton Athletic's 2026/27 Sky Bet Championship campaign with a half-season ticket and save more than £100 compared to purchasing tickets on a match-by-match basis.   <https://www.cafc.co.uk/mens-half-season-ticket-information>
- Click here to secure your 2026/27 season ticket now .   <https://www.cafc.co.uk/mens-half-season-ticket-information>
- 2026/27 Season Ticket Terms & Conditions   <https://www.cafc.co.uk/season-ticket-terms-conditions>
- The following terms and conditions, and the ground regulations displayed in the stadia, govern the use of the 2026/27 season ticket.   <https://www.cafc.co.uk/season-ticket-terms-conditions>
- They define the relationship between the club (Charlton Athletic Football Company Ltd) and the season-ticket holder (the person named on the 2026/27 season ticket), and this takes precedence over all and any previous versions. Use of the season ticket shall be deemed to constitute acceptance of these terms and conditions and ground regulations.   <https://www.cafc.co.uk/season-ticket-terms-conditions>
- 1. The purchase of the season ticket entitles the holder to attend each men’s first-team home league fixture played at The Valley during the 2026/27 season. Entry to cup matches, play-offs, testimonials and friendlies, as well as other events held at the ground, are not permitted via the use of the season ticket.   <https://www.cafc.co.uk/season-ticket-terms-conditions>
- 20. Purchases made on or after March 5th, 2026 shall have no right to cancel their season ticket after that date. The club relies upon the exception under Reg. 6 (2) (b) of the Consumer Protection (Distance Selling) Regulations 2000, under which there is no cooling off period following an applicant’s acceptance.   <https://www.cafc.co.uk/season-ticket-terms-conditions>
- 29. Unplayed fixtures: Season-ticket holders will be able to claim compensation on any home fixtures that are not played in the 2026/27 season. All compensation packages will be processed at the end of the season and all season-ticket holders will be contacted about how to claim.   <https://www.cafc.co.uk/season-ticket-terms-conditions>
- 31. Concession control: For the 2026/27 season, the club will be implementing a strict concession control policy across season tickets. Any supporter found to be using the incorrect age class season ticket may face the possibility of the season ticket in their possession being cancelled.   <https://www.cafc.co.uk/season-ticket-terms-conditions>
